# E1 input data

Generated by `experiments/e1-gps-epoch/steps/95-publish-data.mjs` from `MANIFEST.json`. Do not edit by hand.

Every input E1 reads except the Space-Track element sets. Files in this directory are tracked; the precise-orbit products are release assets, byte for byte as their providers serve them, so each SHA-256 below can be checked against the provider's file.

## Not published

- **Space-Track gp_history element sets.** Space-Track terms: element sets stay on this machine. Each run manifest lists the SHA-256 of every gp_history file read; a Space-Track account can retrieve the same records by CREATION_DATE.
- **Space weather.** E1 does not read space weather (SGP4 element sets; no numerical propagation in H1–H3).
